## Harness work items

- Add a powered crushing-wheel controller at the belt crossing in both movement directions, with full and partially accepting crusher inventories.
- Add a processing machine returning held, unchanged, removed and multiple outputs across centered item ranges.
- Add grouped brass tunnels with every selection mode, blocked/filtered/unloaded outputs, stack splitting, synchronization and saved distribution state.
- Add interrupted chains, unloaded segments, singleton belts and debug-generator worlds; current player-connected chains are fully loaded.
- Add item-processing outputs with empty, single, multiple and held stacks to exercise unchanged-result detection.
- Add living and nonliving passengers, sneaking players with/without cardboard armor, slopes, blockers and riding hierarchies; dropped stacks bypass passenger movement.
- Add living passengers, sneaking/flying players, diving/cardboard boots, rising items, package centering and tunnel-covered landing positions.
- Add merging depots/ejectors, side insertion and fan/press/deployer processing outputs, including full buffers and filters; plain depot histories omit those inputs.
- Add merging incoming stacks, incompatible arrivals, processing HOLD/REMOVE outcomes and funnels; client-only animation guards additionally require a client.
- Add powered weighted ejectors with thresholds, funnels, observers, obstructed trajectories, accepting/full targets and living/package passengers; static stress-load ejectors do not exercise loaded launches and trajectory targets.
- Add weighted ejectors with powered/retracting/charged states, sneaking/bouncing passengers, item landings and friction/fall callbacks; static stress-load ejectors do not exercise passenger landing and bounce histories.
- Add wrench/mirror rotation across all supported axes and rotation amounts; generated placement states do not exercise every rotation callback.
- Andesite junctions hand single items to side depots and belts, and straight tunnels let items through. Remaining: distributing brass tunnels (stuck items, filters, distribution buffers) and unloaded output chunks.
- Belt funnels are placed by state events in consistent shapes. Remaining: player placement and wrenching, adjacent funnels, support changes and collision queries.
- Belt-to-belt, tunnel and depot handoffs from behind and the side are generated. Remaining: vertical or sideways receiving belts, missing controllers or inventories, brass tunnels with distribution above the segment, and simulated insertions.
- Cancelled first selections, cross axes, range, obstacles, cross shafts, opposing driven ends, forgotten shafts and the item cooldown are generated. Remaining: unloaded positions, survival consumption to an empty stack, and rollback through an unbreakable cell.
- Capture cold-start shape-initialization probes; both slope shapes are constructed once before per-case execution data is reset.
- Chutes are placed by state events; player placement (getStateForPlacement), diagonal connections (updateChuteState), the wrench's window/casing changes, industrial iron encasing, canSurvive and shape queries need placement, diagonal and wrench inputs over chutes.
- Collision and outline shapes of diagonal, intersection and encased chutes are built on query; no chute collision query is generated.
- Configure weighted-ejector horizontal/vertical target distances on both sides of the short-distance trajectory threshold; generated stress-load ejectors do not configure trajectory targets.
- Connect brass tunnels with differing axes, removed entities and absent neighbor block entities.
- Create diagonal belts in both slope directions and invoke diagonal-sensitive belt operations; the campaign does not reach both enum outcomes.
- Create random standard and rare packages and query their item ids; generated plain-item histories never select a package style.
- Create/place/open/throw packages with stored contents, addresses, order contexts and charge durations; the transport item contract only permits plain ingots/gems.
- Cross axes, range, obstacles, cross shafts and opposing speeds are generated. Remaining: unloaded ends, vertical-axis diagonals and non-kinetic block entities at a remembered position.
- Distribute unequal and partially accepted stacks across full/filtered outputs using split, forced, random, nearest and round-robin modes.
- Dyes are applied, repeated, changed and washed off; the remaining outcomes are a wash of an undyed belt (water then passes to the bucket, which the generator avoids) and segments without a block entity.
- Dyes, water, casings, covers, pulleys and break cascades are generated on every slope. Remaining: entity passengers and packages, collision and drop queries, schematic requirements, and chains broken by unloaded or missing segments.
- Dyes, water, shafts, casings, the connector and the wrench are used on belts. Remaining: package items, denied-build players, sneaking uses and survival shaft consumption.
- Entities landing on chutes (updateEntityAfterFallOn), empty-hand use, diagonal neighbour updates, removal with a held stack and redstone neighbours need entity, player and diagonal-chute inputs.
- Flap and side discovery with neighbouring tunnels and item transfers are generated and observed. Remaining: the capability query, legacy saved flaps and extended belt funnels.
- Generate real package entities on belts/depots with insertion delays, collision/riding, water, damage and inventory pickup; plain-item inputs do not create packages.
- Initialize/query vertical-belt shape rotations for positive and negative facing axes; shape initialization may occur before per-case probe reset.
- Interior splits (creative), shortening at both ends, lengthening, joins with and without a flip, the maximum length and refusals are generated. Remaining: survival splits with a connector and shafts in the inventory (the actor holds only the used item), incompatible slope joins, missing controllers and cased or pulley segments clicked on other faces.
- Intermediate shafts become pulleys. Remaining: an unbreakable replaceable cell (such as a light block) forcing the rollback.
- Item handler inserts into empty and occupied depots and belt handoffs are generated. Remaining: merging depots and ejectors, oversize stacks and filters.
- Item handler inserts onto a belt segment are generated (capability action). Remaining: extraction, simulated calls, and slots past the segment.
- Land non-item entities and delayed package entities on occupied/accepting depots, or where the input behaviour is absent.
- Land non-item entities and entities at belt/below-belt boundaries, including non-belt targets.
- Loaded sloped chains, repeated drops, reversal, stalls and unequal-speed handoff are generated. Remaining work: side capability insertion and processing locks, which need inventory/processing adapters; BeltInventory.tick tests "currentItem.locked" before ordinary movement.
- Place a pulling funnel over a depot and vary output buffers, filter direction and full/partially accepting destinations.
- Place an ejector using its item, including sneak targeting, and send target settings after placement; generated placements do not include ejectors.
- Place extracting/collecting funnels on all faces; use plain/package stacks, arm/wrench items, item collisions and changing belt supports.
- Place funnel items over belts, depots and ordinary blocks with all facings and failed placement positions.
- Place/power/replace funnels, insert simulated/real stacks into filtered/full/missing inventories and remove supports; current transport contract excludes funnels.
- Placing chute items against chute sides to make diagonal chutes; chutes are placed by state events only.
- Process a depot stack to reach the dirty notification; the true edge is valid gameplay, while the false edge is dead because dirty was unconditionally set true.
- Process multiple near/far items with unchanged, removed, held and multiple-output results.
- Query collision with entity contexts for registered/unregistered passengers across belt slopes.
- Query repeated shape/collision cache hits and all facing/slope/part/casing combinations; current scenarios do not query every collision shape.
- Query standard/rare package ids for the different registered styles; no package item is generated.
- Query the END casing of a sloped belt; its true outcome is reachable, while the false END outcome is impossible after the other three BeltPart values returned.
- Query the END part of a sloped belt; its true outcome is reachable, while the false END outcome is impossible after the other three BeltPart values returned.
- Reachable with a processing-machine adapter, currently absent. BeltProcessingBehaviour.isBlocked returns false for "AbstractFunnelBlock.isFunnel(blockState)" and otherwise checks "getCollisionShape" above the processing space; plain transport worlds cannot invoke processing callbacks.
- Reachable with a processing-result adapter, currently absent. DepotBehaviour.applyToAllItems rejects ".5f - heldItem.beltPosition > maxDistanceFromCentre", calls "processFunction.apply", and routes additional outputs through "processingOutputBuffer".
- Reachable with automation/funnel adapters, currently absent. DirectBeltInputBehaviour.defaultInsertionCallback calls "getCapability(Capabilities.ItemHandler.BLOCK" then "insertItemStacked" with simulate; tryExportingToBeltFunnel requires "Shape.PULLING" and checks "BeltFunnelBlock.POWERED".
- Reachable with press/deployer adapters, currently absent. BeltProcessingBehaviour.ProcessingResult declares "PASS, HOLD, REMOVE"; a plain item drop cannot produce a processing result.
- Read comparators on empty/full/processed depots and a depot behaviour with a zero custom stack limit; snapshots do not query redstone comparator callbacks.
- Reload connected groups with saved distribution targets; the clientPacket visual refresh additionally needs a client.
- Reload dyed/cased controllers and moved belt segments; client-packet visual refresh needs a client.
- Reload funnel cooldown state; the clientPacket refresh arm additionally needs a client.
- Request positions of processed items on horizontal and sloped belts; no processing machine invokes these callbacks.
- Run processing that returns a held output; valid retrieval is unexercised, and retrieving an absent held output is an API-contract error.
- Save pending insertion/removal queues and reload after an end transfer. BeltInventory.write executes "toInsert.forEach(this::insert)" and writes "LazyItem" when "lazyClientItem != null"; tick sets that field on successful server insertion.
- Select unloaded positions and nonshaft blocks with a belt connector.
- Send weighted-ejector target/trigger/elytra/award packets with loaded and unloaded positions; the generator has no ejector packet actions.
- Serialize a fan-processed stack using a registered processing type; the ordinary id path is unexercised, while a missing registration id is rejected as invalid data.
- Set the depot held item through a machine/mounted-storage update while empty and occupied; player use goes through DepotBehaviour instead.
- Side handoffs, slicer rebuilds, dyes and pulley/casing changes are generated. Remaining: removed or missing controllers, unloaded controller chunks, non-item passengers, and processing callbacks.
- Side, retracted and extended andesite funnels take stacks into an empty or occupied depot, holding them when facing against the belt. Remaining: powered funnels, pushing shapes, brass filters and exact/up-to counts, inventory version waits and missing block entities.
- Simulated and real inserts and extracts on every slot are generated. Remaining: output buffer contents (processing outputs) and slot limits asked by other inventories.
- Sloped item histories are generated. Remaining offset/unavailable-segment probes need a query/unloaded-chunk adapter. BeltHelper.getPositionForOffset clamps vertical displacement with "Mth.clamp(offset, 0, controller.beltLength - 1)". Vertical item transport is unavailable in survival: BeltBlock.canTransportObjects returns "slope != BeltSlope.VERTICAL && slope != BeltSlope.SIDEWAYS".
- Smart chutes (redstone power, placement) are not generated.
- Smart chutes with filters, extraction amounts and power are not generated.
- Stone, depots and tunnels above cased belts are generated. Remaining: blocks with narrow or raised collision boxes, crushing wheel controllers and funnels above cased belts.
- Straight brass tunnels are generated; picking up grouped distribution stacks needs distributing brass tunnels, which are not driven.
- Taking from belts and floor funnels taking at belt ends are generated. Remaining: extraction and pushing with cooldowns, filters and count modes, entity collection, and overflowing entities.
- The chute's item handler through the capability action (insert/extract, simulated), not yet aimed at chutes.
- Transfer items in both directions through every tunnel side; the client animation arm additionally needs a client.
- Transport packages, equal-position stacks and fan-processed/locked items, then save/reload them; plain item histories omit these stack metadata combinations.
- Tunnel items are placed on cased and uncased belts. Remaining: placement off a belt or on a sloped belt (canPlace refusals) and obstructed positions.
- Tunnel shapes are queried only by collision; no tunnel collision world is generated.
- Tunnels are placed by the player, take their shapes from side depots, cross belts and neighbouring tunnels, are wrenched and lose their casing or belt. Remaining: rotations, extended belt funnels closing a side, and placement on invalid positions.
- Use brass-tunnel capability insertion/extraction with and without distribution behavior, simulated calls, occupied buffers and absent belt capabilities.
- Use depots from side faces, with a mechanical arm in hand, full/empty output buffers, disabled acceptance and package/non-item landings; query comparator output for empty/full stacks.
- Vertical chutes at belt ends falling into depots, belts and chutes are generated. Remaining: encased fans pushing and pulling columns (calculatePush/calculatePull, air streams, upward output, extractFromBelt, handleInputFromBelow), diagonal and smart chutes, inventories above an empty chute (handleInput, grabCapability), entities landing in chutes, funnels under chutes, and reloads of a moving chute.
- Wrench cased belts and middle pulleys, including survival shaft refunds.
